  8. **Release** — the exact 2-ABI command with the production Worker URL and Razorpay test key builds a **38,405,077-byte** APK under the **40,000,000-byte** gate; package `com.shodasha.shodasha_donor`, label `Shodasha`; merged manifest contains only `INTERNET`, fine/coarse location, network state and the dynamic receiver permission; both ABIs contain the prod URL and key, no loopback default.
- **Why**: The audit's defects were mostly state and money-truth defects, not missing features. Reusing the shared primitives makes every screen’s loading/empty/error/stale/CTA grammar consistent and removes the “which teal?” drift. Keeping the server as the only source for slots, rates, delivery codes, ledger and dues prevents pretty UI from becoming false UI. The 2-ABI gate is the only way to meet the 40MB budget without dropping the release artifact.
- **Consequences**: The release artifact is debug-signed until owner keystore secrets are wired, and production currently returns 404 for `/v1/auth/user/login` (catalog is 200), so the APK cannot authenticate until the owner deploys the 034+ Worker. Local web smoke used `--disable-web-security` + mocked `/v1/**`; it proves layout, not production auth. Physical-device smoke remains owner work. Nothing was pushed, merged to `main`, or deployed.
- **Verification**: `dart format --output=none --set-exit-if-changed lib test` = 67 files/0 changed; `flutter analyze` = No issues found; `flutter test` = **59/59**; `flutter build web --release` succeeded; Playwright mocked 360×800 + 390×844 smoke rendered login/home/orders/tracking without overflow; release APK 38,405,077 bytes, SHA256 `54191550B3FE26BA74B3ED80AD9CDFE83E4D5908E34223DE3963C36D97C525A9`; backend `uv run pytest -q` = **337 passed, 1 skipped**.
- **Affects**: `apps/user_app_donor/lib/**`, `apps/user_app_donor/test/**`, `apps/user_app_donor/android/**`, `apps/user_app_donor/pubspec.*`, `.github/workflows/{ci,release}.yml`, `Feature_docs/user-app-donor/spec-035-premium-ui-states-release.md`, `context/{progress-tracker,flow,decision,ui-context}.md`, branch `035-premium-ui-states-release`

### ADR-130: 035 WS6–WS8 — token-safe transport, transparent recovery, honest cache, donor packaging (2026-10-23)
- **Date**: 2026-10-23
- **Status**: Accepted (WS6–WS8 implemented on `035-premium-ui-states-release`; uncommitted; WS2–WS5 screens not migrated)
- **Context**: The 035 audit found four engineering gaps behind the premium UI work. Transport single-flight keys were method+URL only, so a GET started by user A could be joined by user B after a fast account switch. A 30-minute access expiry forced a cold re-login because `refresh_token` was stored but never used. Logout awaited the server revoke before wiping local state, so a bad network could strand the user in an authenticated shell. The donor app had no CI leg and no release artifact, its Android label still read `Shodasha Donor`, `permission_handler` existed only for `openAppSettings`, and the universal APK (58.5MB measured in 032) exceeded the agreed 40MB budget. WS6–WS8 had to close these without editing feature screens, backend, dependencies beyond the proven removal, or the existing user/vendor pipeline legs.
- **Options considered**:
  1. **Key single-flight by URL only and rely on `clearInflight()` at the auth boundary** — rejected: one missed boundary or a late logout would leak a response across identities; the key itself must be identity-scoped.
  2. **Refresh in each screen/controller** — rejected: duplicated rotation, races, and recursive-401 loops; recovery belongs in one transport hook.
  3. **Cache catalog/windows unconditionally with silent stale fallback** — rejected: stale prices/serviceability would masquerade as fresh; stale is only honest when the screen labels it.
  4. **Ship donor later / keep a universal APK** — rejected: spec decision 1A requires a third side-by-side APK now; a 2-ABI build plus a hard gate is the smallest packaging change that fits the budget.
  5. **Chosen: token-scoped keys + one shared refresh hook + opt-in stale + local-first logout + a third CI/release leg** — each fix sits at the narrowest layer that owns the invariant.
- **Decision**:
  1. **Single-flight identity** — GET keys include a 32-bit FNV-1a fingerprint (token length + hex) only for authed calls. The raw bearer never enters a key, log, or error. `clearInflight()` still runs on every auth boundary in `main.dart` as defence in depth.
  2. **One refresh retry** — `onUnauthorized` is now `Future<bool> Function()?`. On authed 401/403 the client calls it once, retries the original request once only when it returns true, and treats a second rejection as final. Unauthed login/refresh calls never invoke it; `notifyUnauthorized: false` lets auth-management calls opt out. `POST /auth/refresh` is unauthed and never auto-retried (rotation is not replay-safe).
  3. **Recovery ownership** — `AuthController.recoverSession()` shares one in-flight attempt across a burst of 401s, persists the rotated access/refresh pair, and force-logs-out with `sessionExpired` copy on any failure. Cold restore uses the same path when the access token is expired but a refresh token exists. Generation guards prevent a refresh that raced a logout from resurrecting a session.
  4. **Local-first logout** — wipe store/prefs/state first (`isLoggingOut` prevents double-run), then fire-and-forget the revoke with the captured bearer, capped at 3s. The user leaves immediately; a failed revoke never strands a local token.
  5. **Honest cache** — `/catalog` and `/windows` get a 45s TTL keyed by full URI (date + pincode included); an expired entry survives at most 5 minutes and is returned only when the caller passes `allowStale: true` after NETWORK/5xx. Default callers never hide a failed refresh. Wallet, orders, ledger, delivery code, session and complaints are never cached.
  6. **Release URL guard** — `kReleaseMode` rejects loopback hosts (`localhost`, `127.0.0.1`, `10.0.2.2`, `::1`); debug stays permissive.
  7. **Manifest and dependency hygiene** — label `Shodasha`; keep fine/coarse location with optional `android.hardware.location` and `.gps` features; query only `tel`, `mailto`, `https`, `upi`; cleartext only in the debug manifest. No `POST_NOTIFICATIONS`, background location, exact alarms, contacts, SMS or phone-state permissions. Remove `permission_handler` and use `Geolocator.openAppSettings()`; keep `flutter_animate` because `product_detail_sheet.dart` still uses it.
  8. **Pipeline** — `ci.yml` adds exactly one donor leg (`app: donor`, `dir: apps/user_app_donor`, `label: Shodasha (customer parity)`) and inherits analyze/test/debug APK/artifact. `release.yml` adds donor to the same tag with the production Worker URL + Razorpay test key, 2 ABIs (`android-arm,android-arm64`), a 40,000,000-byte gate, and donor download/existence/stage/checksum/body/files entries. `fail-fast: true` keeps publication all-or-nothing. User and vendor legs are untouched.
  9. **Verification** — `dart format` on 9 touched Dart files (0 changed); targeted `dart analyze` clean; `flutter test test/auth_password_test.dart test/api_client_refresh_test.dart test/login_screen_test.dart test/donor_shell_test.dart` **33/33**; both YAMLs parse; both manifests parse. Full analyze/suite and a local donor release build are deferred because WS1/shared-UI edits were concurrent and the owner asked for targeted gates.
- **Why**: Identity belongs in the single-flight key, not only in a boundary call. One owned refresh hook is the only way to rotate tokens without loops or duplicate rotations. A cache that can hide a failed refresh must be opt-in and visibly labelled. Local-first logout matches the user's intent and the security rule that a local token must not survive a logout attempt. A third matrix leg with a hard size gate is minimal pipeline surgery and keeps the three APKs on one tag.
- **Consequences**: A transient network failure during refresh logs the user out (spec behaviour: fail closed). WS2–WS5 screens that want stale catalog/windows must pass `allowStale: true` and render `AppBanner`/`AppStateView.stale`; the default path stays fresh-or-error. The donor APK is 2-ABI only, and CI publishing remains debug-signed until owner keystore secrets are wired — the workflow says so instead of implying production signing. `permission_handler` removal updated `pubspec.lock` and Windows generated plugin registrants. WS6 items 4–5 (shell/wallet ledger fan-out dedupe, subscription-read parallelism) were outside this slice; no money path is cached. Full-suite/analyze confidence is deferred to the WS1→WS2 gate; a local donor release build and measured size are still open. All changes are uncommitted and unpushed.
- **Affects**: `apps/user_app_donor/lib/core/api_client.dart`, `lib/core/auth_impls.dart`, `lib/core/location_service.dart`, `lib/features/auth/auth_controller.dart`, `lib/main.dart`, `android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml`, `android/app/src/debug/AndroidManifest.xml`, `pubspec.yaml`, `pubspec.lock`, `windows/flutter/generated_plugin*`, `test/auth_password_test.dart`, `test/api_client_refresh_test.dart`, `test/login_screen_test.dart`, `test/donor_shell_test.dart`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `.github/workflows/release.yml`, `context/{progress-tracker,flow,decision}.md`, branch `035-premium-ui-states-release`

### ADR-129: 035 WS1 — one token source, compatibility facade, shared UI primitives (2026-10-23)
- **Date**: 2026-10-23
- **Status**: Accepted (WS1 implemented on `035-premium-ui-states-release`; uncommitted; feature screens not yet migrated)
- **Context**: The 035 audits found two competing token systems in `apps/user_app_donor` (`ShodashaTheme` and donor `AppColors`/`AppConstants`), no shared state/CTA grammar, and repeated screen-local button, banner, pill, sheet and empty/error code. WS1 had to establish one foundation before any screen workstream, without changing donor screens, `main.dart`, controllers, transport, workflows or Android in this pass.
- **Options considered**:
  1. **Keep both token systems and restyle screens ad hoc** — rejected: the exact drift WS2–WS8 would inherit, with two sources for the same colours and spacing.
  2. **Adopt a third-party component library** — rejected: new dependency, visual-language mismatch, larger artifact; WS1 is explicitly Material-only.
  3. **One theme source + compatibility facade + a small shared primitive set** — chosen: old names keep compiling while every later workstream imports one truth.
- **Decision**:
  1. **Single token source** — `core/theme.dart` owns the palette, type scale, spacing scale and motion. New semantic tokens: `successDark` `#047857`, `warning` `#B45309`, `link` `#0369A1`, `skeleton` `#E2E8F0`; every pre-existing token stays. Filled money CTAs use `greenDark`; white-on-teal surfaces use `tealDeep`/`tealDark`. Type scale is 12/14/16/18/22/28 at weights 500/600/700/800 using the platform font (no font asset). Focus treatments use an ink ring on filled buttons and `tealDark` borders/`link` text on light surfaces for AA contrast.
  2. **Compatibility facade** — `core/constants/app_constants.dart` keeps every public `AppColors.*` and `AppConstants.*` name but delegates to `ShodashaTheme`/`AppSpacing`/`kMaxContentWidth`. `AppColors.error` maps to the darker accessible `danger`; `success` maps to `success`; `primary` stays brand teal and `primaryDark` maps to `tealDeep`.
  3. **Spacing/motion** — `AppSpacing` = 4/8/12/16/24/32; `kMaxContentWidth = 560`; `AppMotion` = 150/180/220 ms with `AppMotion.reduce(context)` reading `MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)` so every animated primitive has one reduced-motion answer.
  4. **Shared primitives** — `lib/shared/ui/` contains `AppButton` (money/primary/secondary/danger/ghost, busy keeps the fill, disabled is inert, 48dp floor), `AppIconButton` (48dp, tooltip, selected/badge), `AppCard`, `AppSheet` + `showAppSheet`, `AppStateView` (loading skeleton, empty, error, stale, content), `AppBanner` (offline/stale/success), `StatusPill`, `SectionHeader`, `AppTextField`, and a `shared_ui.dart` barrel. Only Material widgets; no package, gradient, emoji, custom font or fake content.
  5. **Scope discipline** — no `lib/shared/state/` yet: WS1 has no second real consumer, so the Ponytail rule says wait. No screen was migrated in this pass; existing imports keep compiling through the facade.
  6. **Verification** — `dart format` on touched files only; `flutter test test/shared_ui_test.dart` **11/11**. Full analyze/suite is deferred to the WS1→WS2 gate per the owner's instruction, so this is foundation-green, not release-green.
- **Why**: One palette and one component grammar stops every later screen fix from inventing a fourth shade or a fifth empty-state shape. A facade rather than a mass rename keeps this pass reviewable and avoids touching screens outside WS1. Building only components with a real consumer avoids a speculative design system. `AppStateView`/`AppBanner` encode the audit's offline-vs-5xx distinction and the "stale must be labelled" law in one place.
- **Consequences**: Later workstreams must import `shared_ui.dart` and `theme.dart`, not donor constants or raw hex. New primitives are untested against real screens until WS2–WS8 migrate them, so API friction may surface then. `AppColors` remains as a temporary compatibility layer; it should shrink as screens migrate and can be removed only after a repo-wide import audit. The `flutter test` run triggered `flutter pub get` because a concurrent change had already removed `permission_handler`; that updated `pubspec.lock` and Windows generated plugin registrants — not WS1-authored and intentionally not reverted.
- **Affects**: `apps/user_app_donor/lib/core/theme.dart`, `apps/user_app_donor/lib/core/constants/app_constants.dart`, `apps/user_app_donor/lib/shared/ui/**`, `apps/user_app_donor/test/shared_ui_test.dart`, `Feature_docs/user-app-donor/spec-035-premium-ui-states-release.md`, `context/{progress-tracker,flow,decision}.md`, branch `035-premium-ui-states-release`

### ADR-128: 034 password auth + 24h delivery code — customer identity without OTP, server-owned handoff secret (2026-10-23)
- **Date**: 2026-10-23
- **Status**: Accepted (implemented on `034-auth-password-delivery-otp`; spec 034 approved by owner 2026-10-22; no push)
- **Context**: 033 shipped a donor-parity customer app on server-code phone login. The owner then locked a different customer identity: name + optional email + Indian mobile + password, with no customer OTP/Firebase. The delivery handoff code also needed a real lifecycle: 24h from dispatch, deletion on use/terminal/expiry, owner reissue, and replay-safe PoD. `States.md` and the 034 audit also required every auth/delivery state and the missing P0/P1 screen states to be honest.
- **Decision**:
  1. **Auth proof** — customer auth is `POST /auth/user/register` and `/auth/user/login` with normalized `+91[6-9]\d{9}` mobile, optional any-provider email, name, and password. There is no customer OTP, no Firebase dependency, no guest session minted by OTP, and no account auto-claim. Existing passwordless rows fail closed until an admin reset. Demo login remains server-gated QA only.
  2. **Passwords** — PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256 at 600,000 iterations, 16-byte salt, 32-byte key, server-side constants only; constant-time compare and a dummy hash for unknown phones. Local Python uses `hashlib`; Cloudflare uses WebCrypto and requires Workers Paid. New migration 026 adds `password_hash`/`password_updated_at`.
  3. **Reset/logout** — `POST /v1/admin/users/{id}/password-reset` is admin-only, audited, and revokes all sessions; no customer self-service reset. Both OTP routes return `410 OTP_RETIRED` without body validation so old clients get the explicit answer. Authed 401/403 calls `forceLogout` locally (no authed retry loop) and shows a one-shot session-expiry notice.
  4. **Delivery-code state machine** — code minted at assignment; 24h TTL starts only at dispatch; states `none|active|expired|locked|unavailable|used`. Codes are cleared on successful use, terminal failure/cancel, expiry, and fifth wrong attempt; expiry timestamps remain as UI tombstones. No deterministic fallback for an active stop. The secret is owner-only, absent from list responses/logs/URLs, and 404 for non-owners.
  5. **Reissue/replay** — owner reissue is allowed only for dispatched, pending stops in `expired|locked|unavailable`, max 3/order/24h, audited with `entity="orders"` + order id. Completed-stop PoD replay returns `{replay:true}` without a code. The scheduler preserves a triple-entered-but-not-PoD-closed stop's code and purges terminal/expired secrets idempotently (migration 027).
  6. **Customer app** — login keeps sign-in/create modes with password visibility, inline validation, 409 pivot, 422 staff copy, generic 401, 429 countdown, offline/5xx split, keyboard inset, support reset dialog and demo QA. Tracking shows code + countdown only while active, explicit expired/locked/unavailable/used copy, local 24h rollover, reissue spinner/errors, stale-refresh banner and app-resume refresh. Order-list cards never show the secret.
  7. **State audit** — the donor app now distinguishes socket failure (`isOffline == code == 'NETWORK'`) from 5xx (`isServerError`) everywhere; 5xx never renders the offline strip. Wallet/profile/notifications/search/address/returns/plan-builder/support/checkout gained the missing honest empty, retry, stale and server-error states. Optional email accepts any valid provider; optional address phone uses the same mobile normalization.
  8. **Verification** — backend **343 passed**; `dart format`, `flutter analyze` clean, `flutter test` **32/32**, debug APK built. Local E2E on `./data/e2e_034.db`: register/login/me, wrong-password 401, OTP 410; demo order unavailable → reissue → vendor PoD → replay true → delivered/used/null code.
- **Why**: Passwords remove the OTP delivery/provider failure mode while keeping a real customer-owned credential; an admin reset is the only safe recovery with no verified channel. The 24h delivery-code lifecycle closes the trust inversion and stale-secret leak in the old app, while preserving the rider handoff. Splitting offline from 5xx stops the UI from lying about connectivity. The state fixes were already approved audit outcomes, not new scope.
- **Consequences**: Cloudflare deployment needs Workers Paid plus owner secrets; old apps fail closed. Existing local DB `workers/api/data/shodasha.db` is stale (`duplicate column name: house`) and was not used. `flutter build windows --debug` failed for missing Visual Studio toolchain (environment). No Android device/emulator smoke, no release signing, no push. `apps/user_app`, vendor/admin/legacy and the untracked phase-C handoff prompt were left untouched.
- **Affects**: `workers/api/src/app/{core/passwords.py, db/migrations/026_user_password.sql, db/migrations/027_pod_otp_lifecycle.sql, services/delivery_code.py, services/auth_service.py, services/order_service.py, services/dispatch_service.py, services/vendor_service.py, jobs/scheduler.py, repositories/user_repo.py, repositories/order_repo.py, api/v1/{auth,admin,orders}.py}`, `apps/user_app_donor/**`, `Feature_docs/user-app-donor/spec-034-auth-password-delivery-otp.md`, `context/{progress-tracker,flow,decision}.md`, branch `034-auth-password-delivery-otp`

### ADR-127: 033 donor-parity customer clone — separate app, real backend, donor screens (2026-10-22)
- **Date**: 2026-10-22
- **Status**: Accepted (implemented on `033-donor-parity`; spec approved by owner before code; no push)
- **Context**: The owner asked for a faithful clone of the donor customer UI and flows, not just a skin transplant, in a **new folder** wired to the existing Shodasha backend, while preserving the current `apps/user_app` as fallback and leaving vendor/admin/legacy untouched. The donor repo (`76e0eba`) has premium customer screens but local fake data, a fake PIN, fake wallet balance/top-up, local addresses and client-side price math. The 032 branch already has the audited real controllers, money laws and state handling.
- **Decision**:
  1. **Branch/folder**: branch `033-donor-parity` from `032-user-app-replacement` tip `f51e06b`; tag `032-baseline`; new customer-only app at `apps/user_app_donor` (`shodasha_donor`, applicationId/namespace `com.shodasha.shodasha_donor`, label `Shodasha Donor`). `apps/user_app` stays in place as fallback; `apps/vendor_app`, `apps/admin_app` and `apps/user_app_legacy_flutter` are untouched.
  2. **Base**: copy the proven 032 core/controllers (`ApiClient`, auth/session, booking/checkout, addresses, orders, subscriptions, wallet, profile, support, returns) and put the donor customer UI on top. One transport, one idempotency/money path, no second API client or local repository.
  3. **Auth**: donor login-first flow using `POST /auth/otp/start` → `POST /auth/otp/verify` with a 6-digit server code. The default Firebase provider path shows an honest “needs configuration” message because this repo has no Firebase client SDK/config; QA uses the server-gated `/auth/demo` door. No fake OTP, no local success, no guest path.
  4. **Screens**: donor top bar, mode cards, featured products (two real SKUs only), static verifiable quality card, five-tab shell (Home / My Orders / Subscriptions / Wallet / Profile), donor order card with Track/Bill only, real ledger wallet (held jars/deposit/dues/receipts, no fake balance or top-up), donor profile, real saved addresses/map picker, support/complaints and notifications derived from real orders/subscriptions. Checkout/search/order actions route through the existing 032 quote/order/payment flow.
  5. **Security/honesty deviations**: staff rider/plant/owner panels stay out; no customer “mark delivered” action; no fake delivery PIN (server PoD code only when returned); no fake wallet top-up/balance; no fake live purity telemetry; no local address list; no client price math.
  6. **Accepted reuse deviations**: Subscriptions, saved addresses and support reuse the real 032 screens/controllers with donor titles/cards/pills instead of cloning the donor's local fake implementations. The donor's `Rs 65` plan modal, hardcoded Bhopal addresses and fake “ticket raised” dialogs are deliberately not cloned; the server plan builder, real address form and real complaint submission are used. Documented in `Feature_docs/user-app-donor/spec.md`.
  7. **Packaging**: cross-platform metadata renamed (web title/manifest, Windows resources, iOS/macOS display/bundle names, Android label, README, pubspec description).
  8. **Tests**: `test/auth_otp_test.dart` (6), `test/donor_order_card_test.dart` (1), `test/login_screen_test.dart` (login smoke at 360dp), `test/donor_shell_test.dart` (five-tab shell smoke at 390dp, fake API) — **10/10 green**. The shell test caught and fixed three real 390dp overflows (featured header, quality header/chip row) with `Expanded`/`Flexible` + ellipsis.
- **Why**: The donor UI is the visual/flow source of truth, but its fake local data cannot ship. Reusing the audited 032 controllers keeps server-owned prices, idempotency, OTP and ledger truth while letting donor screens define the customer experience. A separate app folder preserves the current production fallback and keeps the blast radius to the new tree. The reuse deviations avoid copying fake money/address/ticket code and are explicitly recorded rather than hidden.
- **Consequences**: Gates: `dart format lib test` clean; `flutter analyze` clean; `flutter test` **10/10**; `flutter build apk --debug` succeeded (`app-debug.apk`, 187,748,964 bytes, SHA256 `636F5062A71DFFCC1D13C4C8760DF7BAC5702468576225B54FB2EF195A6E5CFA`). No release APK/signing for this clone; no physical-device smoke; local backend was not running, so no live API smoke. Backend untouched. Open debt: configure a server OTP provider (`fast2sms`/`textbee`) or use the demo door; device smoke for OTP/Razorpay/UPI/GPS/OSM; owner keystore for release. Do not claim release-ready.
- **Affects**: `apps/user_app_donor/**`, `Feature_docs/user-app-donor/spec.md`, `context/{progress-tracker,flow,decision}.md`, branch `033-donor-parity`

### ADR-126: 032 Phase C (P2) — first-run address onboarding, restore fallback, checkout/back guards, transport banner, wide-screen cap and honest list limits (2026-10-22)
- **Date**: 2026-10-22
- **Status**: Accepted (implemented on `032-user-app-replacement`; owner approved the Phase C plan and its three explicit choices: port the first-run sheet, use the existing transport signal instead of a connectivity dependency, and never fake pagination)
- **Context**: ADR-123 left 8 P2 findings after Phases A/B shipped. The owner approved Phase C with these locks: one-time first-run onboarding is ported rather than dropped; offline detection uses the existing `ApiClient` (no new dependency); the shell is capped at 560px on wide screens instead of gaining a tablet layout; and lists whose backend has no cursor must say so honestly.
- **Decision**:
  1. **P2-1 cold-start restore** — `AuthController.restoreSession()` now returns `Future<bool>`, is bounded by an injectable `restoreTimeout` (default 5s), catches secure-store errors, and carries a restore generation so a late completion after the timeout is discarded. `AuthGate` always leaves the splash; a failed/timed-out restore lands on login with the notice “Couldn’t restore your session. Sign in again.”
  2. **P2-2 first-run onboarding** — new `features/addresses/first_run_sheet.dart` with flag `firstrun_done_v1`. `HomeScreen` offers it only when authenticated + `AddrStatus.loaded` + zero addresses + flag unset. The primary action opens the existing Addresses screen (three-step form + OSM pin, no duplicate form); “Not now” and completion both persist the flag. Guests and accounts with addresses never see it. The flag is per install (legacy semantics), not per account.
  3. **P2-3 checkout dismissal** — `showCheckoutSheet` sets `enableDrag:false` and the decorative handle is removed. A `PopScope` intercepts system back/barrier while `_Phase.paying` and shows “Payment still processing” with **Stay** / **Leave**; Leave closes immediately without awaiting the pending POST. Idle sheets still close normally.
  4. **P2-4 conflict and bill truth** — address delete 409 now uses `deleteBlockedBody` (“active orders or subscriptions… complete or cancel them first, or contact support”) instead of the edit-conflict copy. `BillScreen` always renders Cap / Previous dues / Payment; absent values show `—`.
  5. **P2-5 Android back** — the shell wraps its `Scaffold` in `PopScope`: back on any non-Home tab selects Home; back on Home keeps the system exit.
  6. **P2-6 connectivity** — `ApiClient.offline` is a `ValueNotifier<bool>` set true only on timeout/socket failure and false on any HTTP response (4xx/5xx still prove transport). The shell renders an offline strip with Retry (guest-callable `GET /catalog` probe) and a 3s “Back online.” confirmation. The map picker reports tile failures via `errorTileCallback` and retries through a tile-reset stream; the pin/map remain usable.
  7. **P2-7 wide screens** — shell body and bottom navigation are centered and capped at 560 logical px. This is an explicit phone-first decision, not a responsive tablet redesign.
  8. **P2-8 list limits** — when `/payments/me` returns the full 50-row page, Payment History appends “Showing the latest 50 payments…” with the support number; no load-more control is invented because the endpoint has no offset/cursor. Backend audit: `/complaints` returns all rows with no cap (Support builds every returned row in one `ListView` — a future pagination candidate, not a v1 change); `/payments/me` defaults to 50 and caps at 100 with no cursor.
  9. **Tests** — `test/phase_c_states_test.dart` (5: 409 copy, bill rows, cap note, first-run show/persist, Not now/persist) plus 7 tests for restore timeout, checkout dismiss guard, shell back, 560px cap and transport banner. Existing suites updated only where behavior intentionally changed.
- **Why**: These were the remaining state-completeness gaps, not new features. The first-run sheet closes the only legacy flow still missing while reusing the one address form; the restore timeout removes the last infinite-splash path; the paying guard prevents a silent abandoned payment; the transport signal fixes the dead “offline” banner without adding a plugin; and the payment note keeps the 50-row server cap honest instead of pretending to page. No backend contract, money law, theme token, or dependency changed.
- **Consequences**: Gates: `flutter analyze` clean · **222/222** tests (210 Phase B baseline + 12 new) · release APK **55.8MB** (58,553,018 bytes), SHA256 `5C372AAA754BE895E5FD3B61F756CC881B116BACB6295CDF0A0D7D490643C4A0`, built with `SHODASHA_API_BASE=https://water.adityathakur452007.workers.dev/v1` + `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID=rzp_test_Thq4I47cEMIjbF`; per-ABI scan (arm64-v8a / armeabi-v7a / x86_64): prod URL present ✓, `http://localhost:8787` absent ✓, Razorpay test key present ✓. Signature is still the Android debug cert. Backend untouched. Device-verification debt unchanged: Razorpay return, real UPI intent, GPS fixes, live OSM tiles — plus owner keystore/signing. The release stays do-not-ship until that device smoke and signing are done.
- **Affects**: apps/user_app/lib/{core/api_client.dart, features/auth/{auth_controller,auth_gate,name_number_screen}.dart, features/addresses/{address_screen,first_run_sheet,map_picker}.dart, features/booking/{home_screen,booking_sheet}.dart, features/orders/bill_screen.dart, features/wallet/payment_history_screen.dart, features/shell/user_shell.dart}, test/{phase_c_states,auth_gate,checkout_dismiss,api_client,shell}_test.dart, Feature_docs/user-app-replacement/state-audit.md, context/{progress-tracker,flow,decision}.md, branch `032-user-app-replacement`

### ADR-124: 032 Phase A (P0) — session-boundary resets, guest Orders gate, duplicate-order recovery + regression tests (2026-10-21)
- **Date**: 2026-10-21
- **Status**: Accepted (implemented on `032-user-app-replacement`; owner reviews Phase A before Phase B)
- **Context**: ADR-123's audit found 3 P0s at baseline `af1b115`. The owner approved Phase A only ("Cross-account reset, guest Orders gate, duplicate-order recovery + regression tests") and asked for a review point before Phase B.
- **Decision**: (1) **Session boundary at the composition root**: `main.dart` adds an `AuthController` listener that fires only on an authenticated→guest transition and calls `BookingController.resetUserScoped()`, `AddressController.reset()`, `OrdersController.reset()`, `ProfileController.reset()`. Each controller gains a `_generation` counter bumped by `reset()`; every async completion (orders list/loadMore/refreshOrder/cancel/reschedule/rate; addresses load/save/delete; profile load/update) captures the generation and discards its result when stale. Guest Home loads addresses only when `api.hasToken`; the shell reloads them after an in-session guest→login, and post-await ledger writes re-check auth. (2) **Checkout never re-creates**: after `POST /orders` (or `/subscriptions`) succeeds, `placeCheckout` reports payment-step failures as `CheckoutResult.attention`/`attentionMessage` instead of throwing, keeps the controller's idempotency key, and the sheet offers `Retry payment` (re-request intent or re-open the issued payment; same key → server replay) plus `Check Orders`. Poll timeouts offer `Check payment again` + `Check Orders`; no-UPI-app and missing-Razorpay-key keep the created order. An unusable create response (missing id/amount) throws `UnconfirmedCreateException` → close-only copy pointing at Orders/Subscriptions. `CheckoutResult.withIntent()` refreshes the payment handle without losing the created row. (3) **Key rotation policy**: rotate on sheet open and payload changes (steppers, payment mode, delivery type, custom dates); never on retry. (4) **Guest Orders gate**: shell renders `_GuestBody` ("Sign in to see your orders.") exactly like Subscriptions; `OrdersScreen` never mounts for guests. (5) **Recovery UX**: `quote_confirm.dart` attention variant (blue info, amount only when server-confirmed, no deposit/UPI-pending blocks); Orders reloads on tab entry so `Check Orders`/Track order shows the new row. (6) **Tests**: `test/session_reset_test.dart` (reset clears + late-response discard + key policy) and `test/checkout_recovery_test.dart` (service attention with one order POST and same-key intent replay, `UnconfirmedCreateException` with zero intent calls, widget recovery UI with request counts). `shell_test.dart` updated for the guest gate.
- **Why**: These were the only paths that could (a) show another account's data or (b) charge/create twice — direct violations of the privacy and money laws. The chosen shape fixes the root cause (one reset owner at the app root, one create per payload) instead of patching each screen, and uses the server's existing idempotency replay rather than inventing a cancel/compensation endpoint.
- **Consequences**: Gates: `flutter analyze` clean · `flutter test` **197/197 green** (188 + 9 new) · release APK rebuilt with the prod defines **55.7 MB**, SHA256 `F21C4F1CAB9D26C2FBDFF687EF2340915C1643AF2B2FDE4B6B5A161FF70CC380`; per-ABI binary scan: prod URL present ✓, `http://localhost:8787` absent ✓, Razorpay test key present ✓ (signature still the Android debug cert). Backend untouched. Still not device-verified: Razorpay gateway return, real UPI intent, GPS fixes, live OSM tiles. Phase B (14 P1) waits for owner review; Phase C includes the first-run onboarding decision.
- **Affects**: apps/user_app/lib/main.dart, lib/features/booking/{checkout_service,booking_sheet,quote_confirm,booking_controller,home_screen}.dart, lib/features/orders/orders_controller.dart, lib/features/addresses/address_screen.dart, lib/features/profile/profile_controller.dart, lib/features/shell/user_shell.dart, test/{session_reset,checkout_recovery,shell}_test.dart, Feature_docs/user-app-replacement/state-audit.md, context/{progress-tracker,flow,decision}.md

### ADR-125: 032 Phase B (P1) — state completeness across auth, orders, wallet, returns and subscriptions (2026-10-22)
- **Date**: 2026-10-22
- **Status**: Accepted (implemented on `032-user-app-replacement`; owner said “continue” after the Phase A review → Phase B authorized; Phase C still needs explicit approval)
- **Context**: ADR-123’s audit left 14 P1 state findings after ADR-124 fixed the 3 P0s. The owner reviewed Phase A and said “continue”, which authorized Phase B (P1) but not Phase C.
- **Decision**: (1) **Root unwind**: `main.dart` keeps a `GlobalKey<NavigatorState>`; the existing auth-boundary listener now also schedules `popUntil(isFirst)` post-frame when the session ends, so pushed routes/sheets cannot sit above the login screen. Payment History adds a 401/403 “Sign in” fallback CTA wired through Wallet + shell. (2) **Orders truth/staleness**: list-load failures keep cached rows (`status=loaded`) and set `isOffline` only for `ApiException.isNetwork` (offline/5xx); 4xx still errors. `loadMore` is single-flight, dedupes ids across overlapping cursor pages, and exposes `loadMoreFailed`/`retryLoadMore` with an inline footer error; the shell reloads Orders in checkout `onDone` in addition to tab entry. (3) **Tracking/rating**: detail retry calls `refreshOrder(id)` under a local `_detailLoading` instead of reloading the list; reschedule/cancel get distinct success/failure copy. `OrdersController.rate` returns `RateResult {success, alreadyRated, failed}` — 409/`ALREADY_RATED` and the local “already rated” guard are `alreadyRated`; only success/alreadyRated close the sheet, failures keep it open with `rateFailed`. (4) **Estimate/skip**: plan-builder `canSubmit` and `_submit` both require a loaded estimate with no error; skip-today asks for confirmation first. (5) **Freshness**: Wallet gets `RefreshIndicator`, reload after a return request, and a shell `refreshTick` bumped on every Wallet tab entry (covers dues paid in Subscriptions). (6) **Money**: new `lib/core/money.dart` `formatInr` (₹; 2dp only when paise ≠ 0) is the one formatter; `formatRupees` and both `rupeesLabel` helpers delegate; wallet/payment-history stop using `~/ 100`. (7) **Inputs**: return sheet validates the refund VPA with an inline error; address serviceability `null` shows the “couldn’t verify” warning; the address form tracks a dirty baseline and uses `PopScope` to confirm discard on close/system back/drag. Because `PopScope.canPop` is a build-time value, save and confirmed discard route through `_popWith` (setState + post-frame pop) so the guarded route allows the programmatic pop.
- **Why**: These were the remaining state-completeness gaps after the money/privacy P0s: dead ends after session expiry, cached orders that looked live, silent mutation failures, a truncated money format, and forms that lost or accepted invalid input. Fixing them at the root (one navigator key, one formatter, one rating outcome enum, one single-flight page loader) avoids duplicating logic per screen and keeps the locked money laws untouched (no client money math, no new endpoints, no status invention).
- **Consequences**: Gates: `flutter analyze` clean · `flutter test` **210/210 green** (197 + 13: 11 `phase_b_states_test` + 2 subscription widget tests) · release APK rebuilt with the prod defines **55.8 MB**, SHA256 `CC21EB44E4C479E0765B68D7AB9E222D9011AB701D857E2699D837B409B1D244`; APK scan: prod URL present in arm64-v8a/armeabi-v7a/x86_64 ✓, `http://localhost:8787` absent ✓, Razorpay test key present ✓ (signature still the Android debug cert). Design delta: all money now renders with `₹` (was mixed `Rs`/`₹`); subscription tests updated. Backend untouched. Device-verification debt unchanged: Razorpay return, real UPI intent, GPS fixes, OSM tiles, keystore signing. Phase C (8 P2 + the first-run onboarding decision) awaits explicit owner approval.
- **Affects**: apps/user_app/lib/{main.dart, core/money.dart, features/{orders,wallet,returns,addresses,subscriptions,shell,booking}/…}, test/{phase_b_states,order_states,subscription_flow}_test.dart, Feature_docs/user-app-replacement/state-audit.md, context/{progress-tracker,flow,decision}.md, branch `032-user-app-replacement`

### ADR-123: 032 state audit — legacy parity + `States.md` coverage, P0/P1/P2 findings + phased fix plan (2026-10-21)
- **Date**: 2026-10-21
- **Status**: Recorded (audit; **Phase A P0 fixes implemented in ADR-124** — Phase B/C await owner approval)
- **Context**: After S1–S9 shipped, the owner asked to diff the replacement screen-by-screen against the legacy app and the universal `States.md` checklist ("find what is missing in this app… make sure every screen has error/success/next states"). Two read-only explorations (legacy inventory + replacement state coverage) produced `Feature_docs/user-app-replacement/state-audit.md`; every P0/P1 claim was then re-verified by direct source reads at baseline `af1b115`.
- **Decision**: (1) Audit outcome recorded with `file:line` evidence: **3 P0** — (a) `OrdersController`/`AddressController`/`ProfileController`/`BookingController` are app-scoped (`main.dart:81-90`) and never reset on logout/expiry/user switch (`auth_controller.dart:363-396`, `user_shell.dart:93-95`), so account data/dues can bleed across users; (b) a post-create checkout failure rotates the idempotency key on Retry/“Choose COD” (`checkout_service.dart:187-201, 260-269`; `booking_sheet.dart:432-445, 464, 901-920`; `booking_controller.dart:315-321`) → duplicate order or subscription, the top money-law breach; (c) guest Orders tab is ungated and renders cached rows/empty state without a sign-in CTA (`user_shell.dart:311-317`, `orders_screen.dart:59-64, 182-186`). **14 P1** (401 route unwind, unreachable orders offline banner, loadMore guard, stale-after-checkout, tracking feedback, rating errors, plan-builder estimate gate, skip confirmation, wallet refresh + paise truncation, return-UPI validation, address unsaved/unknown serviceability) + **8 P2**. (2) Legacy parity is otherwise complete; the only legacy flow not ported is the first-run locate→address onboarding. (3) Fix plan = **Phase A** P0 + regression tests, **Phase B** P1 states, **Phase C** P2 + first-run decision, each ending analyze/test green, APK rebuild, context sync, commits on `032-user-app-replacement` only.
- **Why**: The current APK passes tests but can duplicate a money action and show another account's data — both violate the locked money/privacy laws, so they must be fixed before any release be it device smoke or store submission. Recording the audit as a decision keeps the "why" traceable and prevents re-litigating the same findings; deferring code to explicit approval honors the design-first protocol.
- **Consequences**: Release APK from `af1b115` is marked **do-not-ship** pending Phase A. Device-verification debt unchanged (Razorpay return, real UPI intent, GPS fixes, OSM tiles, debug-cert signing). No backend changes expected; any COD-recovery change that must cancel a pending order stays within existing API routes (verify against contract first). `flow.md`/`decision.md` flow updates land with the implementation commits, not this audit.
- **Affects**: Feature_docs/user-app-replacement/state-audit.md, context/progress-tracker.md, context/decision.md, branch `032-user-app-replacement`

### ADR-122: 032 slices S2–S9 — donor app fully wired to the Shodasha API + release packaging (2026-10-19)
- **Date**: 2026-10-19
- **Status**: Accepted (design gate closed 2026-10-19; all gates green; branch `032-user-app-replacement`, main untouched)
- **Context**: ADR-121 left a skeleton with five honest placeholders. S2–S9 replaced every one with a live slice against `workers/api`, porting the legacy behavioral suites as slice gates. Work ran as parallel subagent slices (auth/orders/wallet/profile, booking+addresses, subscriptions) followed by one orchestrated integration pass; commits: `80cee31` S2 · `7c06ae2` S5 · `3a58ea1` S7 · `cf2dcac` S8 · `dd24364` shell wiring · `63c91db` S6 · `cb2ebde` shell subs gate · `b27f503` S4 addresses · `a8a1335` S3 booking · `55134be` shell home/ledger/reorder · `8217a46` S9 copy/assets.
- **Decision**: (1) **S2 auth**: donor `AuthGate` owns cold-start restore; `openRegister(context, controller) → Future<bool>` is the one commit gate (guest browse returns false, no dead end); register keeps the server contract (name, `@gmail.com`, 10-digit phone, device) and the config-gated demo sheet. (2) **S3 home/checkout**: `BookingController` takes a `CatalogApi`; only `HttpCatalogApi` (GET /catalog) exists — the legacy `CachingCatalogApi` offline fallback was deliberately dropped because rates are server-owned (incomplete payload → `CatalogUnavailable`; loading/error are honest UI states). Checkout is quote → order → COD|UPI with one idempotency key per payload (fresh key on payment-mode change/session reset); UPI uses only the server intent/link and polls `GET /orders/{id}` ≤5×2s until `payment_status == 'paid_upi'` — the gateway callback alone never settles. (3) **S4 addresses**: `AddressController`/`AddressEntry`/`openAddressesGated` live in `features/addresses/address_screen.dart` (no separate controller file); the login gate re-checks live auth after register; CRUD preserves the legacy payload shape, OSM `flutter_map` pin (no Google key), 409 in-use lock surfaced, `SelectedAddressStore` bound at boot so Home/checkout share one selection. (4) **S5 orders**: live mapping/paging, tracking states, cancel/reschedule/rating/COD-confirm, bill; `CancelReason.label` replaces `labelHi`; reorder hands the order's refill/container lines back to the shell. (5) **S6 subscriptions**: the screen takes `ApiClient` and owns its controller (no shell controller prop); the shell guest-gates the tab so no authed `/subscriptions` request fires before login; dues strip posts `POST /payments/dues-intent` through the sanctioned `ApiClient.send` path with a stable per-amount key, and the returned UPI `link` is never auto-launched (backend may be the fake provider; webhook is settlement truth); server `canceled_at` wins over D1 `paused`; resume/skip/cancel render only server fields. (6) **S7 wallet/returns**: ledger + payment history + returns; the shell injects `ReturnAddressPicker` (maps `AddressEntry.id/label`) so returns imports no address code. (7) **S8 profile/support**: profile identity + PATCH /auth/me edit, payments/addresses rows, support wa.me + complaints through the injected authenticated client, logout via composition root. (8) **Shell composition law**: `UserShell` is the only multi-feature importer. It receives `api`, `bookingController`, `addresses`, `ordersController`, `profileController`, `isAuthenticated()`, `onLoginRequested(BuildContext) → Future<bool>`, `onLogout`, optional `authListenable`, `razorpayKeyId`; it owns cross-tab routes (payments, support, addresses) and the checkout flow so Home's BUY and Orders' reorder share one path; tabs lazy-mount then persist in `IndexedStack`; account-scoped Subscriptions is guest-gated at the shell. Ledger refresh (`GET /ledger/me → BookingController.applyLedger`) runs post-frame and on auth change so the COD gate uses server dues. (9) **Theme**: the global `ElevatedButtonTheme` is the green money CTA and stays full-width (`Size.fromHeight(minTarget)`); inside a horizontally unbounded parent (subscription dues strip `Row`) use a local `minimumSize: Size(0, minTarget)` override rather than weakening every money CTA globally. (10) **Copy**: v1 is English per ADR-120 — auth/orders/bill/tracking/location strings were translated in S9 with assertions updated; map identifiers (`authStringsHi`, `ordersStringsHi`) and keys stay stable for a later Hinglish l10n pass. (11) **Packaging**: `login_hero.png` (2.96 MB) → `login_hero.jpg` (156 KB) and `logo.png` 837 KB → 138 KB (480 px) via GDI+ resample; assets 3.8 MB → 0.31 MB. Release APK built with `--dart-define=SHODASHA_API_BASE=https://water.adityathakur452007.workers.dev/v1 --dart-define=RAZORPAY_KEY_ID=rzp_test_Thq4I47cEMIjbF`.
- **Why**: The donor UI only feels premium if its screens are fed real server truth; every slice therefore kept legacy money laws (server totals, idempotency on money POSTs, OTP server-owned, UPI poll-verified) while adopting donor card/pill/typography grammar. The shell-owned checkout avoids two divergent money paths (Home vs reorder), and injecting callbacks/pickers keeps feature folders import-free except through the shell. Dropping the catalog fallback is safer than showing a stale price, and compressing assets closes the 2.96 MB S1 watch-out without changing layout.
- **Consequences**: Gates: full `flutter analyze` clean · `flutter test` **188/188 green** · release APK **55.7 MB** · per-ABI binary scan: prod URL present in arm64-v8a/armeabi-v7a/x86_64, `http://localhost:8787` absent · backend untouched (baseline 333 passed + 1 known pre-existing `StaleQuoteError` remains owner-owed). Known limits, intentionally honest: no typed `ApiClient.duesIntent` (uses `send`); `createSubscription` exposes no `payment_method`/billing_mode so the builder ships server-default per-delivery COD; `GET /subscriptions` carries no address label/skip history (labels best-effort via `GET /addresses`); logout does not zero the booking controller's last ledger (legacy behavior — server still enforces at commit); Subscriptions screen retains its own 401 state but the shell gates guests before mount. Not device-verified: Razorpay gateway return, real UPI intent handling, GPS fixes, live OSM tiles; release is signed with the Android debug cert until the owner provisions a keystore.
- **Affects**: apps/user_app (lib/main.dart, lib/core/theme.dart, lib/core/location_service.dart, lib/features/{auth,booking,addresses,orders,subscriptions,wallet,returns,profile,support,shell}, assets/*, pubspec.yaml, test/*), context/decision.md, context/progress-tracker.md, context/flow.md, Feature_docs/user-app-replacement/spec.md, branch 032-user-app-replacement

### ADR-121: 032 Slice S1 — repo surgery + donor-based skeleton (theme, transport, boot root, 5-tab shell) (2026-10-19)
- **Date**: 2026-10-19
- **Status**: Accepted (owner go-ahead 2026-10-19 — "continue"; implementation runs on the dedicated branch `032-user-app-replacement`; S1 committed, gates green)
- **Context**: ADR-120's six answers locked the direction. The owner approved continuing and required implementation to start on the dedicated new branch (already created from main `a836fe8` for exactly this mission; main untouched, no commits on main). Slice S1 = repo surgery + theme + transport + boot skeleton per spec §10.
- **Decision**: (1) Repo surgery: `git mv apps/user_app apps/user_app_legacy_flutter` as its own commit (`2f2d47c`, 194 pure renames) — parked reference, zero edits after; the new `apps/user_app` was then reconstructed from the legacy's tracked files only (`git archive` export: platform folders android/ios/linux/macos/web/windows + .gitignore/.metadata/analysis_options + assets wholesale — Kotlin-DSL, R8 minify, adaptive icon, applicationId all preserved), with `lib/`+`test/`+pubspec rebuilt. (2) ONE theme file: `lib/core/theme.dart` keeps the legacy `ShodashaTheme` API surface (same token names/values, `buildShodashaTheme()`) extended with the donor token set (teal family, money-green family, solar accents, eta/blue info, radius ladder 8/12/16/28, card theme); the v2 money laws (green = money CTAs only, blue = links/info only) are documented in-file as non-negotiable. (3) Verbatim ports: `core/api_client.dart` (https/loopback ArgumentError guard, Bearer + X-Device-Id, single-flight GET, keyed-POST retry, `kSupportPhone`), `core/session_store.dart` (incl. `loadOrCreateDeviceId`), `core/auth_impls.dart`, `core/location_service.dart`, `features/auth/auth_controller.dart`. (4) Boot: `main.dart` = composition root (deviceId → ApiClient → AuthController → best-effort `restoreSession()` → shell; `kRazorpayKeyId` dart-define kept) and `features/shell/user_shell.dart` = 5-tab shell (Home/Orders/Subscriptions/Wallet/Profile) with the ADR-112 lazy-mount pattern and honest "Coming soon" placeholders that each slice replaces. (5) Donor `assets/images/login_hero.png` imported for the S2 login hero. (6) `ci.yml` got one comment: legacy folder excluded by omission (matrix paths were already explicit). (7) Slice-boundary deltas vs spec §10: AuthGate moves to S2 (it needs the register screen — S1 boots straight to the shell as guest) and no separate `constants.dart` is created (single-source constants stay co-located where the ports live).
- **Why**: The rename-first commit keeps the legacy tree's history traceable while making the new app buildable from day one; keeping the legacy theme API as a superset means every later slice's ported widget/controller compiles without a token-rename pass, and the donor look arrives through the same single file. Verbatim transport ports preserve the reviewed money-safety semantics (idempotency-key retry, single-flight GET) exactly.
- **Consequences**: Gates: `flutter analyze` clean; `flutter test` 41/41 (api_client, api_parity, auth_validation, demo_login, location_service, theme widget_test, new shell_test). The "Coming soon" placeholders are explicitly temporary — S3/S5/S6/S7/S8 replace them; until then the app is a dev artifact only (no release build shipped). `login_hero.png` is 2.96 MB — an image-optimization pass is scheduled with S9 packaging. Watch-out: `pubspec.lock` re-solved fresh (127 deps, minor bumps vs the legacy lock) — pruning pass at S9 as planned.
- **Affects**: apps/user_app (lib/main.dart, lib/core/*, lib/features/auth/auth_controller.dart, lib/features/shell/user_shell.dart, pubspec.yaml, test/*, assets/images/login_hero.png), apps/user_app_legacy_flutter, .github/workflows/ci.yml, branch 032-user-app-replacement

### ADR-120: 032 design gate — replacement spec + six owner decisions (palette, staff screens, appId, copy, legacy/CI, notifications) (2026-10-19)
- **Date**: 2026-10-19
- **Status**: Accepted (owner answers captured at the design gate 2026-10-19; implementation begins only after explicit spec approval)
- **Context**: ADR-119's handoff mandates a design gate (spec → clarify → approve) before any code. The new session on branch `032-user-app-replacement` re-verified every input (audit 01–06, donor clone @ `76e0eba`, legacy tree + 20-test behavioral spec, CI/release matrices, legacy-vs-donor Android packaging) and produced `Feature_docs/user-app-replacement/spec.md`: site map (donor 5-tab shell + guest browsing), per-file donor→target mapping (adopt-rewire / replace / drop), five user flows with full branch coverage + a money-path sequence diagram, the 5 contract doors as law, a single-theme-file token plan, packaging/repo-surgery/CI plan, and 9 implementation slices (each ending analyze-clean + test-green).
- **Options considered**: palette (donor exactly as coded vs strict legacy teal-v2 vs donor base + v2 money laws); donor-only staff screens (drop vs park source vs hidden debug door); applicationId (`com.shodasha.shodasha_app` upgrade-in-place vs new `com.shodasha.user_app` side-by-side); copy (donor English now vs Hinglish-first); legacy folder/CI (`apps/user_app_legacy_flutter` + exclusion-by-omission vs other naming); notifications screen (drop vs honest empty shell).
- **Decision**: (1) Theme = donor design language as base + legacy v2 money-safety laws (green reserved for money CTAs, blue links/info-badges only), centralized in ONE theme file. (2) Partner/portal login path, rider dashboard, rider delivery, plant hub, owner control center are DROPPED from the customer app (donor GitHub repo + audit docs stay the reference for a future vendor/admin port). (3) applicationId stays `com.shodasha.shodasha_app` (in-place upgrade; no release.yml edits). (4) Donor English copy ships in v1; Hinglish pass is a follow-up phase. (5) `git mv apps/user_app apps/user_app_legacy_flutter`, parked forever, zero edits after the move, excluded from CI by omission (matrix paths are explicit) + one ci.yml comment. (6) Donor notifications screen dropped in v1 (no inbox endpoint; a hardcoded list is worse than none).
- **Why**: Owner answered all six gates preferring the recommended options. Rationale: the donor's brand teal equals palette v2's teal, so "donor base + v2 laws" captures the premium look without losing the money-safety rulings built over ADR-110–118; staff screens cannot function under a `role=user` session (403 by contract) and duplicating vendor/admin surfaces in the customer APK is the exact role-mixing the audit flagged as BLOCKER; keeping the legacy applicationId preserves upgrade paths for installs already in the field; deferring Hinglish keeps the import diff reviewable; explicit CI paths make the legacy exclusion automatic and safe; a phantom inbox would train users to distrust real states.
- **Consequences**: The six answers are now hard constraints for the 9 slices (spec §11). Watch-outs: (a) the legacy 138-test suite no longer gates the shipped app — the new suite ports it per slice; (b) drop decisions are final for the customer app, but the donor source must stay available (GitHub + temp clone) for any later vendor/admin mission; (c) if the appId decision were ever reversed, `release.yml`'s release-body table row + comments must change together (docs currently name `com.shodasha.shodasha_app`).
- **Affects**: Feature_docs/user-app-replacement/spec.md, apps/user_app (to be rebuilt), apps/user_app_legacy_flutter, .github/workflows/ci.yml (comment only), branch 032-user-app-replacement

### ADR-119: Owner pivot — full user-app replacement with donor repo; 031 merged to main (2026-10-19)
- **Date**: 2026-10-19
- **Status**: Accepted (owner-locked direction 2026-10-19; execution delegated to a NEW agent session on branch `032-user-app-replacement`, self-contained prompt at `Feature_docs/user-app-replacement/handoff-prompt.md`)
- **Context**: Owner installed a release APK and still saw the old blue UI. Root cause: `origin/main` sat at `d8da2f1` (2026-10-11, pre-palette-v2) while the entire revamp lived on `031-user-app-ui-revamp` (15 commits: 026 merge ADR-115, palette v2 P0, P1 home, P2a–d reskins, P3 Android icon, ADR-118 hygiene) — and release APKs build from main. Separately, after seeing the skin-transplant result, the owner pivoted: the donor repo's premium look should become the user app wholesale, not a progressive transplant.
- **Decision**: (1) Merge `031-user-app-ui-revamp` into `main` (--no-ff) and push; pre-merge gate re-run on the 031 tip: flutter test 138/138 green. (2) SUPERSEDES the ADR-114 MIX execution path for future work: a new session must `git mv apps/user_app apps/user_app_legacy_flutter` (NEVER delete — the legacy app remains the backend-contract + behavioral-spec reference), import the donor Flutter app (https://github.com/ayushsingh930/Shodasha-Mineral-water) at `apps/user_app` (same folder position so CI paths hold), wire it to `workers/api` (single ApiClient, Bearer + X-Device-Id, Idempotency-Key on money calls, webhook-only UPI verification via order polling, server-owned delivery OTP), and map legacy-only features (server quotes + ₹150 deposit, COD/UPI idempotency, subscriptions, ledger, payments history, returns/refunds, complaints, addresses + OSM picker, profile edit) onto donor screens. Donor's fake-data/no-network patterns (client-minted delivery PIN, fake 4-digit OTP, hardcoded ChangeNotifier singletons) must be deleted, not ported.
- **Why**: Owner's explicit call — the donor UI is "far more premium" and should be the base with our backend making it real. Renaming (not overwriting) preserves the 138-test behavioral spec and 11 contract-true screens as the porting reference while keeping `apps/user_app` in its CI-expected position.
- **Consequences**: The legacy 138-test suite no longer gates the shipped app — the new session must stand up an equivalent test contract for the donor-based app. CI workflow paths must be checked for the renamed legacy folder (exclude it from build/test matrix). Donor-only screens with no backend (portal/donations/owner control-center, rider screens inside a customer app) need an owner clarify call at the design gate. Backend pytest state unchanged (333 passed / 1 pre-documented StaleQuoteError failure, owner-owed per ADR-115).
- **Affects**: apps/user_app (→ apps/user_app_legacy_flutter), apps/user_app (donor import), .github/workflows/release.yml, branch 032-user-app-replacement, Feature_docs/user-app-replacement/handoff-prompt.md

### ADR-118: Contract-audit security-hygiene fixes (https guard, SupportScreen DI, phone single-source) (2026-10-17)
- **Date**: 2026-10-17
- **Status**: Accepted
- **Decision**: From the frontend↔backend contract verification pass (141 backend routes vs full frontend endpoint inventory; pytest 333/1 with the 1 failure being the pre-documented main-only StaleQuoteError), three source-evidenced drift findings were fixed: (1) `ApiClient`'s cleartext `assert` became a construction-time `ArgumentError` — release builds throw unless the base URL is https or an explicit loopback (release APK must be built with `--dart-define=SHODASHA_API_BASE=https://<domain>/v1`; the shipped-inner-device URL cannot silently go cleartext). (2) `SupportScreen` now takes the shell's authenticated `ApiClient` (ADR-112 DI precedent) — the old `ApiClient(accessToken: null)` + `deviceId: 'unknown-device'` fallback is deleted; complaints carry the user's real Bearer + persisted device UUID. (3) The two hardcoded `919302190067` wa.me literals (orders_controller.openWhatsApp, bill_screen._share) derive digits from the single `kSupportPhone` constant.
- **Why**: All three were honest-boundary drift, not exploitable breaks: (1) made the release artifact inert against prod (localhost base) and had no runtime residual guard for owners who set a non-https URL; (2) split the fraud-graph device identity and made the Support tab for logged-in users hit 401-first even when a valid session existed; (3) risked divergence if the support line changes.
- **Consequences**: Debug/test behavior unchanged (loopback allowed; only one test constructs a non-loopback base and it is already https). Watch-outs: future release builds MUST pass the https dart-define; SupportScreen without server/api/controller now fails fast rather than silently mis-identifying. Open (owner-scoped out this pass): `POST /v1/quotes` un-throttled public endpoint; Flutter refresh-token wiring (30-min access expiry → cold re-login).
- **Affects**: apps/user_app/lib/core/api_client.dart, features/support/support_screen.dart, features/shell/user_shell.dart, features/orders/orders_controller.dart, features/orders/bill_screen.dart

### ADR-117: UI revamp P2/P3 batch transplant (donor grammar onto real controllers) + Android brand icon (2026-10-17)
- **Date**: 2026-10-17
- **Status**: Accepted
- **Decision**: Four P2/P3 packages landed on the 031 branch, each tested against the 138-test gate: (1) Profile wallet/ledger region + payment tile rebuilt on donor `wallet_screen.dart` grammar (solid-teal hero card — donor's gradient flattened per no-gradient law — caps label, w900 amount) fed ONLY by the already-wired `ProfileController.load()` → `ApiClient.ledgerMe()` → `GET /v1/ledger/me` (`LedgerSnapshot`: depositPaise/duesPaise/heldJars); loading/error states render spinner or retry card so a fake ₹0 hero can never flash; donor's Add-Money/wallet-balance fakes omitted (no wallet concept in our backend). Address screen adopts the donor `top_bar.dart` DELIVERY-TO card look (caps header + live count "N pate", teal selection treatment 1.5px+tint+check, type icon, Default badge on teal not blue); OSM picker + controller untouched. (2) `booking_sheet.dart` reskinned on donor `cart_checkout_sheet.dart` — drag-handle header, teal-tint icon cards for address/schedule, extra-strong price card (string/value bytes identical), sticky pinned CTA strip with hairline border, `close` disabled while busy; all frozen methods (`_pay/_collectUpi/_verifyOrPollPayment/_onGateway*` etc.) byte-identical (diff-verified); `_PayChip` selected fill moved blue→teal (blue is link-only). (3) Orders/tracking/subscriptions/support reskinned on donor `order_card.dart` + live `subscription_screen.dart` grammar — white cards on slate ground, status pills mapped to OUR status set only (teal in-flight, slate delivered, danger failed), reorder = green money CTA, dues strip keeps green money CTA, delivery-OTP reveal logic/strings byte-identical; 3 emoji glyphs dropped from banner strings (zero-emoji law, no test asserts them, copy otherwise verbatim); honest additive strings only (2 map keys: address count "N pate", "Active" pill for real active state). (4) Android adaptive icon: hand-written vector `ic_launcher_foreground_brand.xml` (white drop + teal inner echo, 2 paths, safe-zone centered), `ic_launcher_background` → #0D9488 in existing colors.xml (was #FFFFFF), `mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml` foreground swapped to brand drawable (inset wrapper dropped — vector is already safe-zone centered), new `ic_launcher_round.xml`, manifest gains `android:roundIcon`; legacy mipmap PNGs kept as API<26 fallback.
- **Why**: Fill the 02-parity P2 queue with donor craft while the skin stays presentation-only — every number rendered comes from a real server fetch, every action stays on our controllers; palette v2 rulings applied strictly (teal structure, green money-only, blue link-only; `Colors.white` on the cancel button replaced by `ShodashaTheme.surface` token). Idempotency/UPI/tracking logic was explicitly diff-frozen because a checkout/taxonomy string change would break the 138-test contract.
- **Consequences**: Screens visually converge on the donor's premium grammar without any trust inversion. Watch-outs: pre-26 launchers keep the default PNG icon (binary PNG replacement is an optional owner follow-up); wallet history remains on `PaymentHistoryScreen` (GET /payments/me via existing tile navigation — no controller edit); de-emoji reads as a copy polish, not a rename.
- **Affects**: apps/user_app/lib/features/profile/profile_screen.dart, addresses/address_screen.dart, booking/booking_sheet.dart, orders/orders_screen.dart, orders/tracking_screen.dart, subscriptions/subscription_screen.dart, support/support_screen.dart, apps/user_app/android/** (branch 031-user-app-ui-revamp)

### ADR-116: Register skin transplant (donor login skeleton) + cta/focus color rulings (2026-10-17)
- **Date**: 2026-10-17
- **Status**: Accepted
- **Decision**: `name_number_screen.dart` (register, POST /v1/auth/user/register) reskinned on the friend's `login_screen.dart` skeleton — welcome band (white sheet on slate ground: brand mark, 22/w900 wordmark, trust line, fact chips centered), eyebrow(11/w800/ls0.4)→w900 headline header, label-above fields over theme borders with a teal 2px focus ring, +91-hairline phone prefix (flag emoji dropped), flat 52dp ink CTA; `first_run_screen.dart` consistency pass (icons blue→teal, w900 titles, note blue→muted, sheet radius 8→token). Zero user-facing string or logic changes.
- **Why**: Donor elements without real data (portal tabs, 4-box OTP, role-detection card, hero gradient, green CTA) are omitted per the no-fake-data law and palette v2: green = money CTAs only (register is not money), teal = structure/focus (the v1 blue-only `focusedBorder` was a law violation, now teal 2px matching home's selected-card width precedent), so the CTA keeps the screen's locked ink primary — same literal #0F172A as the donor's dark partner CTA. Labels move above fields per donor grammar; `find.text('Email')` cardinality stays 1 (label is now an external Text, labelText removed from decoration), so behavior tests stay green.
- **Consequences**: Register screen matches donor rhythm while palette v2 stays legal; chips centered via Wrap (no narrow-screen overflow); first-run sheet aligned with register grammar. Watch: if a later wave changes the CTA color systemically, do it via theme tokens, not screen-local styleFrom (this file's two remaining blue TextButtons are legal links).
- **Affects**: apps/user_app/lib/features/auth/name_number_screen.dart, apps/user_app/lib/features/auth/first_run_screen.dart (branch 031-user-app-ui-revamp, uncommitted)

### ADR-114: User-App UI Import Strategy — Mix plan (owner-locked 2026-10-17)
- **Date**: 2026-10-17
- **Status**: Accepted (owner locked the Mix plan 2026-10-17; exec details: replace home/login visuals + wallet/address cards on friend skeletons, restyle the rest; temp-dir reference; teal #0D9488 + green #16A34A; English copy now, Hindi pass later). Rationale line replaced friend's skeletons rewired to our controllers, restyle the rest; friend's repo = temp-dir reference only; teal #0D9488 brand + green #16A34A CTA; keep friend's English copy for now, Hindi pass later)
- **Context**: Owner wants the premium UI of friend's repo `ayushsingh930/Shodasha-Mineral-water` (Flutter, ~5,500 lines, Firebase/Firestore-declared) applied to the user app. Friend's repo was cloned read-only to temp; 5 parallel audit agents + synthesis produced `Feature_docs/user-app-ui-import-audit/01-06`. Findings: friend's app has NO network layer (0 HTTP clients, 0 real Firestore calls despite `cloud_firestore` in pubspec), all state in 5 hardcoded in-memory ChangeNotifier singletons; auth is fake 4-digit OTP accepting any code; delivery PIN is client-minted and printed to the rider (trust inversion); customer app embeds rider + owner control-center screens (role mixing); packaging uses `com.example.shodasha`, default launcher icon, debug-signed release, dead deps with 0 imports, ~1,008 lines dead code + duplicate subscription screens; 0 of 7 committed conversion goals present (no one-tap repeat defaults, no ₹150 deposit disclosure, no UPI/COD, no Hindi, no WhatsApp). Craft worth keeping: dense order-card grammar, gradient heroes, status pills, 4-box OTP row, radii/typography rhythm.
- **Options considered**: (A) Replace `apps/user_app` with friend's repo and rewire — rejected: inherits dead code/deps/stub tests, loses 138 green tests and 11 contract-true screens, slower than rebuilding. (B) **Skin transplant** — import visual design language into our screens wired to existing BookingController/ApiClient contracts — RECOMMENDED. (C) Reuse friend's modules — only ~8 widgets qualify as clipboard donors.
- **Decision**: Option B (proposed). Phased plan P0 design-language lock → P1 branch `031-user-app-ui-revamp` + theme/shell → P2 core-flow reskins (home, checkout, tracking, auth, profile/wallet, subscriptions) with tests green per flow → P3 Android packaging polish of OUR app → P4 verification & merge. Friend's repo to be kept as reference-only (temp clone), NOT vendored; avoids the `rm -rf .git` step entirely.
- **Why**: Our feature set (Bearer register, server quote math, Idempotency-Key checkout, UPI poll, tracking OTP, ledger, returns, subscriptions) is the differentiator and the friend's UI implements none of it; transplanting the skin keeps contracts intact with shortest diff.
- **Consequences**: None yet — audit docs only. Blocked on owner answers to 6 open questions in doc 06 (option confirm, vendoring, teal-vs-green, copy language, rider/owner screens fate, PR split).
- **Affects**: `Feature_docs/user-app-ui-import-audit/*`, branch `031-user-app-ui-revamp` (created 2026-10-17).
- **Execution (2026-10-17, on branch 031-user-app-ui-revamp)**: 026 merged into main first (see ADR-115). P0 theme lock landed (teal/green/slate + radius 12; widget_test contract updated). P1 home reskin landed (teal schedule cards, caps eyebrows, w900 headline, teal quote strip, teal plus-buttons; all strings kept so behavior tests stay green, the intentionally renamed `SAB SAMAAN` eyebrow updated in grocery_lift_test). 138/138 user tests green + analyze clean at every commit. Next: login skin + wallet/address cards on friend skeletons, checkout/tracking restyles, then Android packaging (P3).

### ADR-113: Bhopal Water Supply Vendors Research & Field Intelligence Directory
- **Date**: 2026-10-12
- **Status**: Accepted
- **Context**: Ground-level operations for Shodasha Mineral Waters and competitor analysis required verified field intelligence on water supply vendors operating strictly within Bhopal municipal & suburban limits (specifically Kolar Road, Arera Colony, MP Nagar, Katara Hills, Govindpura, Karond, Saket Nagar, Misrod, and TT Nagar).
- **Decision**: Compiled and verified 28 local water vendors (packagers, 20L jar delivery services, bulk water tanker providers, and municipal civic helplines) cross-verified against official Bhopal Municipal Corporation (BMC) registries, Bhopal Helpline (`bhopalhelpline.com`), IndiaMART Bhopal, Justdial Bhopal, and Webindia123 Yellow Pages. Stored in structured table format at `bhopal_water_supply_vendors.md` and `context/bhopal_water_supply_vendors.md`.
- **Why**: Provides accurate local market intelligence on supplier names, brand identities, operational territories, and active contact numbers without relying on unverified external assumptions.
- **Consequences**: Enables accurate vendor onboarding, supply partner prospecting, and benchmark analysis for 20L jar pricing across all Bhopal zones.
- **Affects**: `bhopal_water_supply_vendors.md`, `context/bhopal_water_supply_vendors.md`, field operations, vendor onboarding.

### ADR-112: Background Query Optimization, Lazy Tab Rendering, Payment History Auth Injection & Cloudflare D1 Payments Composite Index
- **Date**: 2026-10-11
- **Status**: Accepted
- **Context**:
  1. *Payment History 401 Loop & Error Loads*: When users clicked "Mere Payments / Hisaab" in `ProfileScreen` or `OrdersScreen`, `PaymentHistoryScreen` was instantiated without an `api` instance (`const PaymentHistoryScreen()`). It fell back to `ApiClient()`, which lacked a session token getter. Consequently, `GET /v1/payments/me?limit=50` was sent with no `Authorization: Bearer` header, failing with 401 UNAUTH on the server. The screen rendered "Payments load nahi hue" with a "Dobara try karein" button, causing repeated rapid unauthenticated requests in seconds.
  2. *Background Query Storm on User App Boot*: `UserShell` utilized an eager `IndexedStack` that mounted all four tabs (Home, Orders, Support, Profile) simultaneously on boot. This fired 5-6 concurrent network queries on app launch (`/catalog`, `/addresses`, `/ledger/me`, `/orders`, `/auth/me`), causing request storms on serverless workers and executing unauthenticated background queries in guest mode.
  3. *Unindexed D1 Table Scans on Payments*: `PaymentRepo.list_user_payments` queries `payments` filtered by `user_id` and ordered by `created_at DESC`. `payments` only had indexes on `order_id` and `status`, forcing full-table scans during payment history lookups.
- **Decision**:
  1. *Authenticated Dependency Injection in User App (`apps/user_app`)*:
     - Injected `api: widget.api` from `UserShell` into `OrdersScreen` and `ProfileScreen`.
     - Forwarded `api: widget.api ?? _c.api` to `PaymentHistoryScreen` from both `ProfileScreen` and `OrdersScreen`.
     - In `ApiClient`, added public getter `hasToken`.
     - In `PaymentHistoryScreen`, added an upfront `_api.hasToken` check to display an honest unauthenticated message rather than firing a failing network call, and added clear session-expired messaging on 401/403.
  2. *Lazy Tab Mounting (`apps/user_app`)*:
     - Implemented `_visitedTabs` in `UserShell` state. Unvisited tabs render `const SizedBox.shrink()` inside `IndexedStack`, mounting only when the user explicitly navigates to them.
     - In `OrdersScreen.initState`, guarded `controller.load()` behind `isAuthenticated` to prevent background 401 errors for guests.
  3. *Cloudflare D1 Composite Index (`workers/api`)*:
     - Created migration `025_payments_user_index.sql`: `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_user_created ON payments (user_id, created_at DESC)`.
     - Added `test_payments_me_endpoint_http_auth_and_unauth` in `test_payments_me.py` to ensure endpoint returns 401 unauthenticated and 200 with data for authenticated users.
- **Consequences**:
  - Eliminates the 401 error and retry loop when opening "Mere Payments".
  - Reduces initial app startup network requests from 6 down to 1-2 (Home catalog/address only).
  - Eliminates full table scans in D1 for user payment history.
  - All test suites green: 138 user app tests, 70 vendor app tests, 328 backend pytests, clean admin build.
- **Affects**:
  - `apps/user_app/lib/main.dart`
  - `apps/user_app/lib/core/api_client.dart`
  - `apps/user_app/lib/features/shell/user_shell.dart`
  - `apps/user_app/lib/features/profile/profile_screen.dart`
  - `apps/user_app/lib/features/profile/payment_history_screen.dart`
  - `apps/user_app/lib/features/orders/orders_screen.dart`
  - `apps/user_app/test/payment_history_test.dart`
  - `workers/api/src/app/db/migrations/025_payments_user_index.sql`
  - `workers/api/tests/test_payments_me.py`

### ADR-111: Doorstep COD Auto-Reconciliation on PoD, Multi-Jar Mixed Cart Settlement, Repeat Subscription Deposit Waiving, and Vendor Custody Visibility
- **Date**: 2026-10-11
- **Status**: Accepted
- **Context**:
  1. *Silent Unreported COD Vulnerability*: In `apps/vendor_app`, the rider entered delivery OTP and cash in `PodSheet`. The backend `pod_complete` stored the cash in JSON metadata (`stops.triple['pod']['cash']`) but never called `PaymentRepo.mark_paid_cash_locked()` and never incremented `vendor_profile.in_hand`. Orders remained unpaid, customer ledgers retained dues, and physical cash held by vendors went untracked unless riders separately clicked a separate "Cash" button on the stop card.
  2. *Multi-Jar Mixed Cart Nuances*: Customers frequently order diverse combinations (e.g. 3-4 containers + 3-4 refills/without containers) or return partial empties. In recurring subscriptions, repeat daily deliveries represent replenishment (refills) rather than purchasing new physical containers every day. In `scheduler.py`, repeat cycle generation needed to pass `deposit_already_paid_paise` so repeat subscriptions are not billed duplicate deposits.
  3. *Vendor In-Hand Custody vs Labor Payouts*: The system separates Water Order Total (100% agency revenue), Vendor In-Hand Custody (`vendor_profile.in_hand`, physical agency money held by the driver), and Vendor Labor Fee (`payouts` table, `stops_done * per_stop_fee`). Admin payout generation and approval required explicit exposure of `in_hand` custody balance to prevent payout disbursement to vendors with outstanding unreturned agency cash.
- **Decision**:
  1. *Auto-Reconcile Doorstep COD on PoD (`workers/api` & `apps/vendor_app`)*:
     - In `vendor_service.py:pod_complete`, under `WRITE_LOCK`, if an order is unpaid and cash is submitted (`cash_paise > 0`), automatically invoke `PaymentRepo.mark_paid_cash_locked(stop["order_id"], cash_paise, vendor_id)` and increment `vendor_profile.in_hand += cash_paise` in the same atomic transaction.
     - In `apps/vendor_app/lib/features/stops/pod_sheet.dart`, `initState` prefills `_cash.text` with the order's rupee amount when `collectPaise > 0` and mode is `cod`, reducing friction for delivery drivers while allowing adjustment for partial amounts.
  2. *Multi-Jar Mixed Cart & Subscription Deposit Waiving (`workers/api`)*:
     - Confirmed multi-item SKU quote generation handles arbitrary mixed line items (`n_refill * 28 + n_container * 30`) and computes net uncovered container deposits (`max(0, n_container - empties - covered_jars) * 150`). Added comprehensive test in `test_quotes.py`.
     - In `scheduler.py`, fetch `deposit_paid` from `LedgerRepo` and pass `deposit_already_paid_paise=dep_paid` to `pricing.compute_quote`, ensuring active subscriptions never double-bill security deposits on recurring runs.
  3. *Admin In-Hand Custody Visibility on Payouts (`workers/api`)*:
     - In `admin.py:payout_generate` and `payout_approve`, attach `in_hand_custody` to the payout payload and audit log, giving back-office operators immediate visibility into unreturned agency cash before approving payouts.
- **Why**: Eliminates cash leakage, closes the silent COD gap, guarantees clean multi-jar pricing and subscription repeatability, and provides unified custody reconciliation across the three money dimensions.
- **Consequences**: Doorstep COD payments immediately clear customer dues and book vendor cash custody upon OTP completion; all 327 backend pytests, 70 vendor app tests, 134 user app tests, and admin web builds pass 100% green.
- **Affects**: `workers/api` (`vendor_service.py`, `scheduler.py`, `admin.py`, `test_vendor.py`, `test_quotes.py`), `apps/vendor_app` (`pod_sheet.dart`), `context/`

### ADR-110: Core Flow Bug Fixes: UPI Deep-Link Verification & Polling, COD Idempotency Auto-Refresh, Customer Profile Editing (Name/Phone/Gmail), Security Deposit Visibility, Customer-Vendor Binding Auto-Routing, and Subscription Activation
- **Date**: 2026-10-11
- **Status**: Accepted
- **Context**:
  1. *UPI Intent / Google Pay Bug & Premature Order Completion*: When the user selected UPI, the app opened a hardcoded `shodasha@upi` dummy deep-link instead of backend provider intent. Google Pay threw "account not found or account not configured". When the user returned to the app, `launchUrl` returning true or Razorpay client callbacks immediately triggered `widget.onDone(result)`, falsely declaring the order completed even though payment was unconfirmed or failed.
